The validator therefore received an empty fixed-code payload (none of the expected file separators are present).

3. **The reproduction test doesn't even target the provided files.** It greps `camel/models/deepseek_model.py` for the snippet:

   ```
   messages = [
                   {  # type: ignore[misc]
                       **msg,
                       'content': re.sub(
                           r'thinking...'
   ```
